## SPEC MIV (canônico — index.css + /miv/ showcase)
Paleta: void #000 (bg) · **cyan #00F2FF** (sistema/interativo/primário/estrutural) · **gold #D4AF37** (valor/mcoCoins/ROI — só valor) · **nebula #8C5AFF** (memória/conhecimento) · bone (texto branco/opacidade).
Tokens de STATUS do MIV (index.css --status-*) — MANTER, são on-brand: verde #22c55e (sucesso/ativo) · vermelho #ef4444 (erro) · amber/gold #f59e0b (aguardando/pendente). NÃO trocar cor semântica de status.
Só o DECORATIVO off-palette vira MIV: violet/fuchsia/purple/pink/magenta → cyan (ou nebula se for contexto de memória); azul genérico (#3b82f6/blue-*) → cyan; laranja decorativo (não-waiting) → gold ou cyan.

## COMO CONVERTER emoji
- Emoji inline em JSX (ex: `<span>🎬</span>` ou `🎬 CINEMA`): troque por `<Film className="h-4 w-4 ..." />` (importe o ícone). Mantenha o texto ao lado.
- Emoji em ARRAY de dados (ex: `icon: '🚀'`) consumido por render `{item.icon}` NO MESMO arquivo: troque `icon: '🚀'`→`icon: Rocket` (componente) e o render `{item.icon}`→`<item.icon className="h-4 w-4" />`. Se o `icon` é PERSISTIDO em store/JSON ou o render está em OUTRO arquivo que você NÃO possui, use string-nome + um map local `{ rocket: Rocket }` no arquivo de render (padrão do `src/components/orchestrator/NodeIcon.tsx` — leia-o como referência). Emoji de PLATAFORMA redundante com label de texto (ex: "🌐 WP") pode virar só o texto.

## OBRIGATÓRIO antes de retornar
Rode `npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | grep -E "<cada arquivo seu>"` e CONFIRME zero erros nos SEUS arquivos (corrija até limpar). NÃO rode build. Edite só os arquivos da sua lista. Retorne o resumo (schema).

> 152	/** Get user session token for edge function auth */
> 153	async function getAuthToken(): Promise<string> {
> 154	  const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 155	  if (!session?.access_token) throw new Error("Você precisa estar logado para usar esta funcionalidade.");
> 156	  return session.access_token;
> 157	}
> 158	
> 159	/** Stream SSE from edge function using user's auth token */
> 160	async function streamFromEdge(
> 161	  fnName: string,
> 162	  body: object,
> 163	  onChunk: (text: string) => void,
> 164	): Promise<string> {
> 165	  const token = await getAuthToken();
> 166	  const resp = await fetch(
> 167	    `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/${fnName}`,
> 168	    {
> 169	      method: "POST",
> 170	      headers: {
> 171	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 172	        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
> 173	      },
> 174	      body: JSON.stringify(body),
> 175	    }
> 176	  );
> 177	
> 178	  if (!resp.ok) {
> 179	    const err = await resp.json();
> 180	    throw new Error(err.error || "Erro na geração");
> 181	  }
> 182	
> 183	  const reader = resp.body?.getReader();
> 184	  if (!reader) throw new Error("No stream");
> 185	  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
> 186	  let buffer = "";
> 187	  let result = "";
> 188	
> 189	  while (true) {
> 190	    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
> 191	    if (done) break;
> 192	    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
> 193	
> 194	    let nl: number;
> 195	    while ((nl = buffer.indexOf("\n")) !== -1) {
> 196	      let line = buffer.slice(0, nl);
> 197	      buffer = buffer.slice(nl + 1);
> 198	      if (line.endsWith("\r")) line = line.slice(0, -1);
> 199	      if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
> 200	      const jsonStr = line.slice(6).trim();
> 201	      if (jsonStr === "[DONE]") break;
> 202	      try {
> 203	        const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
> 204	        const content = parsed.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
> 205	        if (content) {
> 206	          result += content;
> 207	          onChunk(result);
> 208	        }
> 209	      } catch {}
> 210	    }
> 211	  }
> 212	  return result;
> 213	}

> 441	        {

> 451	        const err = await resp.json();

> 454	      const data = await resp.json();
> 455	      setImgPreview(data.base64);

> 483	    if (!audioPrompt.trim()) return;

> 485	    setAudioResult("");
> 486	    try {
> 487	      await streamFromEdge("generate-audio-script", {
> 488	        prompt: audioPrompt,
> 489	        format: audioFormat,
> 490	        duration: audioDuration,
> 491	      }, setAudioResult);
> 492	    } catch (e: any) {

> 494	    } finally {

> 498

> 501	    createContent.mutate({
> 502	      user_id: user.id,
> 503	      type: "audio",

> 505	      body: audioResult,

> 516	  const handleVideoGenerate = async () => {
> 517	    if (!videoPrompt.trim()) return;
> 518	    setVideoGenerating(true);
> 519	    setVideoResult("");
> 520	    try {
> 521	      await streamFromEdge("generate-video-script", {
> 522	        prompt: videoPrompt,
> 523	        format: videoFormat,
> 524	        duration: videoDuration,
> 525	        provider: scriptProvider,
> 526	        model: scriptModel,

> 534	
> 535	  /** Auto-save script to content_library and return the record ID */
> 536	  const autoSaveScript = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
> 537	    if (!videoResult || !user) return null;
> 538	    try {
> 539	      const { data } = await supabase.from("content_library").insert({
> 540	        user_id: user.id,
> 541	        type: "video",
> 542	        title: `${videoFormat.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + videoFormat.slice(1)}: ${videoPrompt.slice(0, 60)}`,
> 543	        body: videoResult,
> 544	        ai_prompt: videoPrompt,
> 545	        ai_model: `${videoProvider}/${videoModel}`,
> 546	        tags: [videoFormat, "vídeo-ia", videoProvider],
> 547	        status: "draft",
> 548	      }).select("id").single();
> 549	      return data?.id || null;
> 550	    } catch { return null; }
> 551	  };
> 552	
> 553	  const handleSaveVideo = () => {
> 554	    if (!videoResult || !user) return;
> 555	    if (videoContentId) {
> 556	      // Already saved — just update with media_url if available
> 557	      if (videoUrl) {
> 558	        supabase.from("content_library")
> 559	          .update({ media_url: videoUrl, status: "ready" })
> 560	          .eq("id", videoContentId)
> 561	          .then(() => toast({ title: "Vídeo salvo na biblioteca!" }));
> 562	      } else {
> 563	        toast({ title: "Roteiro já salvo na biblioteca!" });
> 564	      }
> 565	    } else {
> 566	      createContent.mutate({
> 567	        user_id: user.id,
> 568	        type: "video",
> 569	        title: `${videoFormat.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + videoFormat.slice(1)}: ${videoPrompt.slice(0, 60)}`,
> 570	        body: videoResult,
> 571	        media_url: videoUrl || undefined,
> 572	        ai_prompt: videoPrompt,
> 573	        ai_model: `${videoProvider}/${videoModel}`,
> 574	        tags: [videoFormat, "vídeo-ia", videoProvider],
> 575	        status: videoUrl ? "ready" : "draft",
> 576	      });
> 577	    }
> 578	    stopWatcher();
> 579	    setVideoOpen(false);
> 580	    setVideoPrompt("");
> 581	    setVideoResult("");
> 582	    setVideoUrl(null);
> 583	    setVideoRenderStatus("");
> 584	    setVideoOperationId(null);
> 585	    setVideoProgress(0);
> 586	    setVideoContentId(null);
> 587	  };
> 588	
> 589	  /** Stop The Watcher polling */
> 590	  const stopWatcher = useCallback(() => {
> 591	    if (watcherRef.current) {
> 592	      clearInterval(watcherRef.current);
> 593	      watcherRef.current = null;
> 594	    }
> 595	  }, []);
> 596	
> 597	  /** Start The Watcher — frontend polling for video completion */
> 598	  const startWatcher = useCallback((opId: string, prov: string, contentId: string | null) => {
> 599	    stopWatcher();
> 600	    setVideoProgress(5);
> 601	    setVideoRenderStatus("Sincronizando com GPUs...");
> 602	    let tick = 0;
> 603	
> 604	    watcherRef.current = setInterval(async () => {
> 605	      tick++;
> 606	      // Simulated progress ramp
> 607	      const simulated = Math.min(90, 5 + tick * 3);
> 608	      setVideoProgress(simulated);
> 609	
> 610	      if (simulated < 20) {
> 611	        setVideoRenderStatus("Sincronizando com GPUs...");
> 612	      } else if (simulated < 85) {
> 613	        setVideoRenderStatus(`Renderizando frames cinematográficos... (Estimativa: 3-5 min)`);
> 614	      } else {
> 615	        setVideoRenderStatus("Finalizando e salvando no Storage GCRUX...");
> 616	      }
> 617	
> 618	      try {
> 619	        const token = await getAuthToken();
> 620	        const resp = await fetch(
> 621	          `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/check-video-status`,
> 622	          {
> 623	            method: "POST",
> 624	            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 625	            body: JSON.stringify({ operation_id: opId, provider: prov, content_id: contentId }),
> 626	          }
> 627	        );
> 628	        const data = await resp.json();
> 629	
> 630	        if (data.status === "completed" && data.video_url) {
> 631	          setVideoProgress(100);
> 632	          setVideoRenderStatus("Vídeo gerado com sucesso!");
> 633	          setVideoUrl(data.video_url);
> 634	          setVideoRendering(false);
> 635	          stopWatcher();
> 636	          toast({ title: "✅ Vídeo pronto!", description: "Renderização concluída." });
> 637	        } else if (data.status === "failed") {
> 638	          setVideoProgress(0);
> 639	          setVideoRenderStatus("");
> 640	          setVideoRendering(false);
> 641	          stopWatcher();
> 642	          toast({ title: "Falha na renderização", description: data.error, variant: "destructive" });
> 643	        }
> 644	      } catch {
> 645	        // Network error — keep polling
> 646	      }
> 647	    }, 15000); // Poll every 15s
> 648	  }, [stopWatcher, toast]);
> 649	
> 650	  // Cleanup watcher on unmount
> 651	  useEffect(() => () => stopWatcher(), [stopWatcher]);
> 652	
> 653	  /** Fire-and-forget: submit video job + activate The Watcher */
> 654	  const handleVideoRender = async () => {
> 655	    if (!videoResult) return;
> 656	    setVideoRendering(true);
> 657	    setVideoUrl(null);
> 658	    setVideoProgress(0);
> 659	    setVideoRenderStatus("Enviando roteiro ao provedor...");
> 660	
> 661	    // Auto-save script first
> 662	    let contentId = videoContentId;
> 663	    if (!contentId) {
> 664	      contentId = await autoSaveScript();
> 665	      if (contentId) setVideoContentId(contentId);
> 666	    }
> 667	
> 668	    try {
> 669	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 670	      const resp = await fetch(
> 671	        `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/generate-video`,
> 672	        {
> 673	          method: "POST",
> 674	          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 675	          body: JSON.stringify({
> 676	            prompt: videoPrompt,
> 677	            script: videoResult.slice(0, 2000),
> 678	            provider: videoProvider,
> 679	            model: videoModel,
> 680	            content_id: contentId,
> 681	          }),
> 682	        }
> 683	      );
> 684	      const data = await resp.json();
> 685	
> 686	      if (data.operation_id) {
> 687	        setVideoOperationId(data.operation_id);
> 688	        toast({ title: "🚀 Renderização iniciada", description: `Operação: ${data.operation_id.slice(-12)}` });
> 689	        startWatcher(data.operation_id, data.provider || videoProvider, contentId);
> 690	      } else if (data.error) {
> 691	        setVideoRendering(false);
> 692	        setVideoRenderStatus("");
> 693	        toast({ title: "Erro", description: data.error, variant: "destructive" });
> 694	      }
> 695	    } catch (e: any) {
> 696	      setVideoRendering(false);
> 697	      setVideoRenderStatus("");
> 698	      toast({ title: "Erro", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" });
> 699	    }
> 700	  };
> 701	
> 702	  const copyToClipboard = (text: string) => {
> 703	    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
> 704	    toast({ title: "Copiado para a área de transferência!" });
> 705	  };
> 706	
> 707	  const handleSEOAnalyze = async (contentId: string, text: string) => {
> 708	    setSeoContentId(contentId);
> 709	    setSeoOpen(true);
> 710	    setSeoAnalyzing(true);
> 711	    setSeoResult(null);
> 712	    try {
> 713	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 714	      const resp = await fetch(
> 715	        `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/analyze-seo`,
> 716	        {
> 717	          method: "POST",
> 718	          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 719	          body: JSON.stringify({ text }),
> 720	        }
> 721	      );
> 722	      if (!resp.ok) {
> 723	        const err = await resp.json();
> 724	        throw new Error(err.error || "Erro na análise");
> 725	      }
> 726	      const data = await resp.json();
> 727	      setSeoResult(data);
> 728	    } catch (e: any) {
> 729	      toast({ title: "Erro na análise SEO", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" });
> 730	      setSeoOpen(false);
> 731	    } finally {
> 732	      setSeoAnalyzing(false);
> 733	    }
> 734	  };
> 735	
> 736	  const handleApplySEO = () => {
> 737	    if (!seoResult?.optimized_text || !seoContentId) return;
> 738	    updateContent.mutate({ id: seoContentId, body: seoResult.optimized_text });
> 739	    setSeoOpen(false);
> 740	    setSeoResult(null);
> 741	    toast({ title: "Conteúdo otimizado aplicado!" });
> 742	  };
> 743	
> 744	  const ML_URL_RE = /https?:\/\/(?:[\w-]+\.)?mercadolivre\.com\.br\/[^\s"'),<>]+/g;
> 745	
> 746	  const handleMonetizeLinks = async (item: Content) => {
> 747	    if (!item.body || !user) return;
> 748	    setMonetizingId(item.id);
> 749	    try {
> 750	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 751	      const matches = item.body.match(ML_URL_RE);
> 752	      if (!matches || matches.length === 0) {
> 753	        toast({ title: "Nenhum link do Mercado Livre encontrado no conteúdo" });
> 754	        return;
> 755	      }
> 756	      const unique = [...new Set(matches)];
> 757	      let updatedBody = item.body;
> 758	      let okCount = 0;
> 759	      let healCount = 0;
> 760	
> 761	      for (const url of unique) {
> 762	        const resp = await fetch(
> 763	          `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/process-affiliate-link`,
> 764	          {
> 765	            method: "POST",
> 766	            headers: {
> 767	              "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 768	              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
> 769	            },
> 770	            body: JSON.stringify({ product_url: url, content_id: item.id }),
> 771	          },
> 772	        );
> 773	        if (!resp.ok) continue;
> 774	        const data = await resp.json();
> 775	        if (data.affiliate_url) {
> 776	          updatedBody = updatedBody.split(url).join(data.affiliate_url);
> 777	          okCount++;
> 778	          if (data.self_healed) healCount++;
> 779	        }
> 780	      }
> 781	
> 782	      if (okCount === 0) {
> 783	        toast({ title: "Nenhum link processado com sucesso", variant: "destructive" });
> 784	        return;
> 785	      }
> 786	
> 787	      const newTags = [
> 788	        ...new Set([...(item.tags ?? []).filter((t) => t !== "afiliado-ml"), "afiliado-ml"]),
> 789	      ];
> 790	      updateContent.mutate({ id: item.id, body: updatedBody, tags: newTags });
> 791	      toast({
> 792	        title: `${okCount} link(s) monetizado(s) com sucesso`,
> 793	        description:
> 794	          healCount > 0
> 795	            ? `${healCount} produto(s) recuperado(s) via self-heal`
> 796	            : undefined,
> 797	      });
> 798	    } catch (e: any) {
> 799	      toast({
> 800	        title: "Erro ao monetizar links",
> 801	        description: e.message,
> 802	        variant: "destructive",
> 803	      });
> 804	    } finally {
> 805	      setMonetizingId(null);
> 806	    }
> 807	  };
> 808	
> 809	  const typeIcon = (type: string) => {
> 810	    switch (type) {
> 811	      case "image": return <Image className="h-4 w-4" />;
> 812	      case "video": return <Video className="h-4 w-4" />;
> 813	      case "audio": case "music": return <Music className="h-4 w-4" />;
> 814	      default: return <FileText className="h-4 w-4" />;
> 815	    }
> 816	  };
> 817	
> 818	  // TTS player
> 819	  const handleTTS = useCallback((itemId: string, text: string) => {
> 820	    if (ttsPlaying === itemId) {
> 821	      synthRef.current.cancel();
> 822	      setTtsPlaying(null);
> 823	      return;
> 824	    }
> 825	    synthRef.current.cancel();
> 826	    // Clean script markers for better TTS
> 827	    const cleaned = text.replace(/\[.*?\]/g, "").replace(/\n{2,}/g, "\n");
> 828	    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(cleaned);
> 829	    utterance.lang = "pt-BR";
> 830	    const voices = synthRef.current.getVoices();
> 831	    const ptVoice = voices.find(v => v.lang.startsWith("pt"));
> 832	    if (ptVoice) utterance.voice = ptVoice;
> 833	    utterance.onend = () => setTtsPlaying(null);
> 834	    utterance.onerror = () => setTtsPlaying(null);
> 835	    setTtsPlaying(itemId);
> 836	    synthRef.current.speak(utterance);
> 837	  }, [ttsPlaying]);
> 838	
> 839	  const stopTTS = useCallback(() => {
> 840	    synthRef.current.cancel();
> 841	    setTtsPlaying(null);
> 842	  }, []);
> 843	
> 844	  return (
> 845	    <div className="space-y-6">
> 846	      {/* Header */}
> 847	      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
> 848	        <div>
> 849	          <h1 className="text-2xl font-heading font-bold text-foreground">
> 850	            Biblioteca de Conteúdo
> 851	          </h1>
> 852	          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-1">
> 853	            Crie, organize e gerencie seus conteúdos com IA
> 854	          </p>
> 855	        </div>
> 856	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
> 857	          {/* AI Generate */}
> 858	          <Dialog open={aiOpen} onOpenChange={setAiOpen}>
> 859	            <DialogTrigger asChild>
> 860	              <Button className="gap-2 glow-primary">
> 861	                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />
> 862	                Gerar com IA
> 863	              </Button>
> 864	            </DialogTrigger>
> 865	            <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto">
> 866	              <DialogHeader>
> 867	                <DialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
> 868	                  <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
> 869	                  Gerar Conteúdo com IA
> 870	                </DialogTitle>
> 871	              </DialogHeader>
> 872	
> 873	              <div>
> 874	                <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Templates rápidos</Label>
> 875	                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-2">
> 876	                  {TEMPLATES.map((t, i) => (
> 877	                    <Button
> 878	                      key={i}
> 879	                      variant={selectedTemplate === i ? "default" : "outline"}
> 880	                      size="sm"
> 881	                      className="text-xs justify-start"
> 882	                      onClick={() => {
> 883	                        setSelectedTemplate(i);
> 884	                        setAiPrompt(t.prompt + " ");
> 885	                        setAiPlatform(t.platform);
> 886	                      }}
> 887	                    >
> 888	                      {t.name}
> 889	                    </Button>
> 890	                  ))}
> 891	                </div>
> 892	              </div>
> 893	
> 894	              <div className="space-y-4 mt-2">
> 895	                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
> 896	                  <div>
> 897	                    <Label>Tom de voz</Label>
> 898	                    <Select value={aiTone} onValueChange={setAiTone}>
> 899	                      <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
> 900	                      <SelectContent>
> 901	                        {TONES.map((t) => (
> 902	                          <SelectItem key={t.value} value={t.value}>{t.label}</SelectItem>
> 903	                        ))}
> 904	                      </SelectContent>
> 905	                    </Select>
> 906	                  </div>
> 907	                  <div>
> 908	                    <Label>Plataforma</Label>
> 909	                    <Select value={aiPlatform} onValueChange={setAiPlatform}>
> 910	                      <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Opcional" /></SelectTrigger>
> 911	                      <SelectContent>
> 912	                        <SelectItem value="linkedin">LinkedIn</SelectItem>
> 913	                        <SelectItem value="instagram">Instagram</SelectItem>
> 914	                        <SelectItem value="twitter">Twitter/X</SelectItem>
> 915	                        <SelectItem value="tiktok">TikTok</SelectItem>
> 916	                        <SelectItem value="youtube">YouTube</SelectItem>
> 917	                        <SelectItem value="facebook">Facebook</SelectItem>
> 918	                      </SelectContent>
> 919	                    </Select>
> 920	                  </div>
> 921	                </div>
> 922	
> 923	                <div>
> 924	                  <Label>Prompt</Label>
> 925	                  <Textarea
> 926	                    value={aiPrompt}
> 927	                    onChange={(e) => setAiPrompt(e.target.value)}
> 928	                    placeholder="Descreva o conteúdo que deseja gerar..."
> 929	                    rows={3}
> 930	                  />
> 931	                </div>
> 932	
> 933	                <Button
> 934	                  onClick={handleAIGenerate}
> 935	                  disabled={aiGenerating || !aiPrompt.trim()}
> 936	                  className="w-full gap-2"
> 937	                >
> 938	                  {aiGenerating ? (
> 939	                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
> 940	                  ) : (
> 941	                    <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />
> 942	                  )}
> 943	                  {aiGenerating ? "Gerando..." : "Gerar Conteúdo"}
> 944	                </Button>
> 945	
> 946	                {aiResult && (
> 947	                  <div className="rounded-lg border bg-muted/30 p-4 space-y-3">
> 948	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
> 949	                      <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Resultado</Label>
> 950	                      <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => copyToClipboard(aiResult)}>
> 951	                        <Copy className="h-3 w-3 mr-1" />
> 952	                        Copiar
> 953	                      </Button>
> 954	                    </div>
> 955	                    <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-foreground">
> 956	                      {aiResult}
> 957	                    </div>
> 958	                  </div>
> 959	                )}
> 960	              </div>
> 961	
> 962	              <DialogFooter>

> 1199	                        <SelectContent>
> 1200	                          {(TEXT_MODELS[scriptProvider] || []).map((m) => (

> 1202	                          ))}

> 1204	                      </Select>

> 1374	        </div>

> 1376	          <SelectTrigger className="w-[130px]"><SelectValue placeholder="Tipo" /></SelectTrigger>

> 1397	      {/* Content Grid */}
> 1398	      {isLoading ? (

> 104	  HOOK: 'border-l-amber-500',

> 109	
> 110	function parseScenes(body: string): Scene[] {
> 111	  const lines = body.split('\n');
> 112	  const scenes: Scene[] = [];
> 113	  let current: Scene | null = null;
> 114	  let currentSection = '';
> 115	
> 116	  for (const line of lines) {
> 117	    const sceneMatch = line.match(/^\[?(CENA|INTRO|HOOK|ENCERRAMENTO|CTA)\s*\d*\]?[:\s-]*(.*)/i);
> 118	    if (sceneMatch) {
> 119	      if (current) scenes.push(current);
> 120	      const type = sceneMatch[1].toUpperCase();
> 121	      current = {
> 122	        id: `scene-${scenes.length}`,
> 123	        type,
> 124	        label: sceneMatch[2]?.trim() || `${type} ${scenes.length + 1}`,
> 125	        narration: '',
> 126	        onScreen: '',
> 127	        duration: '10s',
> 128	      };
> 129	      currentSection = '';
> 130	      continue;
> 131	    }
> 132	
> 133	    if (!current && line.trim()) {
> 134	      current = {
> 135	        id: `scene-${scenes.length}`,
> 136	        type: 'CENA',
> 137	        label: `Cena ${scenes.length + 1}`,
> 138	        narration: '',
> 139	        onScreen: '',
> 140	        duration: '10s',
> 141	      };
> 142	    }
> 143	
> 144	    if (!current) continue;
> 145	
> 146	    const lower = line.toLowerCase().trim();
> 147	    if (lower.startsWith('narração:') || lower.startsWith('narrador:') || lower.startsWith('áudio:')) {
> 148	      currentSection = 'narration';
> 149	      current.narration += line.replace(/^(narração|narrador|áudio):\s*/i, '') + '\n';
> 150	    } else if (lower.startsWith('texto em tela:') || lower.startsWith('visual:') || lower.startsWith('tela:')) {
> 151	      currentSection = 'onScreen';
> 152	      current.onScreen += line.replace(/^(texto em tela|visual|tela):\s*/i, '') + '\n';
> 153	    } else if (lower.startsWith('duração:') || lower.startsWith('tempo:')) {
> 154	      current.duration = line.replace(/^(duração|tempo):\s*/i, '').trim();
> 155	    } else if (line.trim()) {
> 156	      if (currentSection === 'onScreen') {
> 157	        current.onScreen += line + '\n';
> 158	      } else {
> 159	        current.narration += line + '\n';
> 160	      }
> 161	    }
> 162	  }
> 163	  if (current) scenes.push(current);
> 164	
> 165	  return scenes.map((s) => ({
> 166	    ...s,
> 167	    narration: s.narration.trim(),
> 168	    onScreen: s.onScreen.trim(),
> 169	  }));
> 170	}
> 171	
> 172	function scenesToText(scenes: Scene[]): string {
> 173	  return scenes.map((s) => {
> 174	    let text = `[${s.type}] ${s.label}\n`;
> 175	    if (s.narration) text += `Narração: ${s.narration}\n`;
> 176	    if (s.onScreen) text += `Texto em tela: ${s.onScreen}\n`;
> 177	    text += `Duração: ${s.duration}`;
> 178	    return text;
> 179	  }).join('\n\n');
> 180	}

> 277	  const [videoRendering, setVideoRendering] = useState(false);
> 278	  const [operationId, setOperationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
> 279	  const [contentStatus, setContentStatus] = useState<string>('');
> 280	  const watcherRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
> 281	
> 282	  useEffect(() => {
> 283	    if (content?.body) {
> 284	      setScenes(parseScenes(content.body));
> 285	    }
> 286	  }, [content?.body]);
> 287	
> 288	  // On-mount: Fetch latest status directly from DB to bypass stale cache
> 289	  useEffect(() => {
> 290	    async function fetchLatestState() {
> 291	      if (!id) return;
> 292	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 293	        .from('content_library')
> 294	        .select('status, media_url, operation_id, metadata')
> 295	        .eq('id', id)
> 296	        .maybeSingle();
> 297	
> 298	      if (data && !error) {
> 299	        setContentStatus(data.status || '');
> 300	        setAssets((((data as any).metadata)?.assets as AssetEntry[]) || []);
> 301	        if (data.media_url) {
> 302	          setVideoUrl(data.media_url);
> 303	        }
> 304	        if (data.operation_id && data.status === 'processing') {
> 305	          console.log(`Detectada operação em curso: [${data.operation_id}]. Retomando monitoramento...`);
> 306	          setOperationId(data.operation_id);
> 307	          // Auto-start watcher immediately since we confirmed it's processing
> 308	          if (!videoUrl && !videoRendering) {
> 309	            startWatcher(data.operation_id);
> 310	          }
> 311	        }
> 312	      }
> 313	    }
> 314	    fetchLatestState();
> 315	    
> 316	    return () => stopWatcher();
> 317	  }, [id]);
> 318	
> 319	  const getAuthToken = async () => {
> 320	    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
> 321	    return data.session?.access_token || '';
> 322	  };
> 323	
> 324	  const fetchProviderModels = useCallback(async (provider: string) => {
> 325	    if (modelsCacheRef.current[provider]) {
> 326	      setDiscoveredModels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: modelsCacheRef.current[provider] }));
> 327	      return;
> 328	    }
> 329	
> 330	    setModelsLoading(true);
> 331	    setModelsError(null);
> 332	    try {
> 333	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 334	      const resp = await fetch(
> 335	        `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/list-provider-models`,
> 336	        {
> 337	          method: "POST",
> 338	          headers: {
> 339	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 340	            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
> 341	          },
> 342	          body: JSON.stringify({ provider }),
> 343	        }
> 344	      );
> 345	      const data = await resp.json();
> 346	
> 347	      if (data.error === "key_missing") {
> 348	        setModelsError(`Chave não configurada para ${data.provider}. Vá em Configurações.`);
> 349	        const fallback = FALLBACK_MODELS[provider] || [];
> 350	        setDiscoveredModels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: fallback }));
> 351	        return;
> 352	      }
> 353	
> 354	      if (data.models && data.models.length > 0) {
> 355	        const mapped = data.models.map((m: any) => ({ value: m.id, label: m.name }));
> 356	        modelsCacheRef.current[provider] = mapped;
> 357	        setDiscoveredModels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: mapped }));
> 358	      } else {
> 359	        const fallback = FALLBACK_MODELS[provider] || [];
> 360	        setDiscoveredModels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: fallback }));
> 361	      }
> 362	    } catch {
> 363	      const fallback = FALLBACK_MODELS[provider] || [];
> 364	      setDiscoveredModels((prev) => ({ ...prev, [provider]: fallback }));
> 365	    } finally {
> 366	      setModelsLoading(false);
> 367	    }
> 368	  }, []);
> 369	
> 370	  useEffect(() => {
> 371	    fetchProviderModels(videoProvider);
> 372	  }, [videoProvider, fetchProviderModels]);
> 373	
> 374	  const handleGenerateScript = async () => {
> 375	    if (!videoPrompt.trim()) return;
> 376	    setScriptGenerating(true);
> 377	    
> 378	    try {
> 379	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 380	      const resp = await fetch(
> 381	        `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/generate-video-script`,
> 382	        {
> 383	          method: "POST",
> 384	          headers: {
> 385	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
> 386	            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
> 387	          },
> 388	          body: JSON.stringify({
> 389	            prompt: videoPrompt,
> 390	            format: videoFormat,
> 391	            duration: videoDuration,
> 392	            provider: videoProvider,
> 393	            model: videoModel
> 394	          }),
> 395	        }
> 396	      );
> 397	
> 398	      if (!resp.ok) {
> 399	        const err = await resp.json();
> 400	        throw new Error(err.error || "Erro na geração do roteiro");
> 401	      }
> 402	
> 403	      const reader = resp.body?.getReader();
> 404	      if (!reader) throw new Error("No stream");
> 405	      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
> 406	      let buffer = "";
> 407	      let fullResult = "";
> 408	
> 409	      while (true) {
> 410	        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
> 411	        if (done) break;
> 412	        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
> 413	
> 414	        let nl: number;
> 415	        while ((nl = buffer.indexOf("\n")) !== -1) {
> 416	          let line = buffer.slice(0, nl);
> 417	          buffer = buffer.slice(nl + 1);
> 418	          if (line.endsWith("\r")) line = line.slice(0, -1);
> 419	          if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
> 420	          const jsonStr = line.slice(6).trim();
> 421	          if (jsonStr === "[DONE]") break;
> 422	          try {
> 423	            const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
> 424	            const contentChunk = parsed.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content;
> 425	            if (contentChunk) {
> 426	              fullResult += contentChunk;
> 427	            }
> 428	          } catch {}
> 429	        }
> 430	      }
> 431	      
> 432	      const newScenes = parseScenes(fullResult);
> 433	      setScenes(newScenes);
> 434	      toast({ title: "Roteiro gerado com sucesso!" });
> 435	      
> 436	    } catch (e: any) {
> 437	      toast({ title: "Erro na geração", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" });
> 438	    } finally {
> 439	      setScriptGenerating(false);
> 440	    }
> 441	  };
> 442	
> 443	  const stopWatcher = useCallback(() => {
> 444	    if (watcherRef.current) {
> 445	      clearInterval(watcherRef.current);
> 446	      watcherRef.current = null;
> 447	    }
> 448	  }, []);
> 449	
> 450	  const startWatcher = useCallback((opId: string) => {
> 451	    stopWatcher();
> 452	    setVideoRendering(true);
> 453	    setVideoProgress(5);
> 454	    setVideoRenderStatus('Sincronizando com GPUs...');
> 455	    let tick = 0;
> 456	
> 457	    // Detect provider from operation_id format
> 458	    const provider = opId.startsWith('models/') ? 'gemini' : 'openrouter';
> 459	
> 460	    watcherRef.current = setInterval(async () => {
> 461	      tick++;
> 462	      const simulated = Math.min(90, 5 + tick * 3);
> 463	      setVideoProgress(simulated);
> 464	
> 465	      if (simulated < 20) {
> 466	        setVideoRenderStatus('Sincronizando com GPUs...');
> 467	      } else if (simulated < 85) {
> 468	        setVideoRenderStatus('Renderizando frames cinematográficos... (Estimativa: 3-5 min)');
> 469	      } else {
> 470	        setVideoRenderStatus('Finalizando e salvando no Storage GCRUX...');
> 471	      }
> 472	
> 473	      try {
> 474	        const token = await getAuthToken();
> 475	        const resp = await fetch(
> 476	          `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/check-video-status`,
> 477	          {
> 478	            method: 'POST',
> 479	            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 480	            body: JSON.stringify({ operation_id: opId, provider, content_id: id }),
> 481	          }
> 482	        );
> 483	        const data = await resp.json();
> 484	
> 485	        if (data.status === 'completed' && data.video_url) {
> 486	          setVideoProgress(100);
> 487	          setVideoRenderStatus('Vídeo gerado com sucesso!');
> 488	          setVideoUrl(data.video_url);
> 489	          setVideoRendering(false);
> 490	          setContentStatus('ready');
> 491	          stopWatcher();
> 492	          toast({ title: '✅ Vídeo pronto!', description: 'Renderização concluída.' });
> 493	        } else if (data.status === 'failed') {
> 494	          setVideoProgress(0);
> 495	          setVideoRenderStatus('');
> 496	          setVideoRendering(false);
> 497	          setContentStatus('failed');
> 498	          stopWatcher();
> 499	          toast({ title: 'Falha na renderização', description: data.error, variant: 'destructive' });
> 500	        }
> 501	      } catch {
> 502	        // Keep polling on network errors
> 503	      }
> 504	    }, 15000);
> 505	  }, [stopWatcher, toast, id]);
> 506	
> 507	  const handleRetriggerRender = async () => {
> 508	    if (!content || !id) return;
> 509	    setVideoRendering(true);
> 510	    setVideoUrl(null);
> 511	    setVideoProgress(0);
> 512	    setVideoRenderStatus('Reenviando roteiro ao provedor...');
> 513	
> 514	    try {
> 515	      const token = await getAuthToken();
> 516	      const resp = await fetch(
> 517	        `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/generate-video`,
> 518	        {
> 519	          method: 'POST',
> 520	          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
> 521	          body: JSON.stringify({
> 522	            prompt: content.ai_prompt || content.title,
> 523	            script: scenesToText(scenes).slice(0, 2000),
> 524	            provider: (content.ai_model || '').split('/')[0] || 'gemini',
> 525	            model: (content.ai_model || '').split('/').slice(1).join('/') || 'veo-3.1-generate-preview',
> 526	            content_id: id,
> 527	          }),
> 528	        }
> 529	      );
> 530	      const data = await resp.json();
> 531	
> 532	      if (data.operation_id) {
> 533	        setOperationId(data.operation_id);
> 534	        setContentStatus('processing');
> 535	        toast({ title: '🚀 Renderização iniciada', description: `Operação: ${data.operation_id.slice(-12)}` });
> 536	        startWatcher(data.operation_id);
> 537	      } else if (data.error) {
> 538	        setVideoRendering(false);
> 539	        setVideoRenderStatus('');
> 540	        toast({ title: 'Erro', description: data.error, variant: 'destructive' });
> 541	      }
> 542	    } catch (e: any) {
> 543	      setVideoRendering(false);
> 544	      setVideoRenderStatus('');
> 545	      toast({ title: 'Erro', description: e.message, variant: 'destructive' });
> 546	    }
> 547	  };
> 548	
> 549	  const handleSceneChange = useCallback((updated: Scene) => {
> 550	    setScenes((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === updated.id ? updated : s)));
> 551	  }, []);
> 552	
> 553	  const handleDragEnd = (event: DragEndEvent) => {
> 554	    const { active, over } = event;
> 555	    if (!over || active.id === over.id) return;
> 556	    setScenes((prev) => {
> 557	      const oldIdx = prev.findIndex((s) => s.id === active.id);
> 558	      const newIdx = prev.findIndex((s) => s.id === over.id);
> 559	      const copy = [...prev];
> 560	      const [moved] = copy.splice(oldIdx, 1);
> 561	      copy.splice(newIdx, 0, moved);
> 562	      return copy;
> 563	    });
> 564	  };
> 565	
> 566	  const handleSave = async () => {
> 567	    if (!id) return;
> 568	    setSaving(true);
> 569	    try {
> 570	      await updateContent.mutateAsync({ id, body: scenesToText(scenes) });
> 571	    } finally {
> 572	      setSaving(false);
> 573	    }
> 574	  };
> 575	
> 576	  const handleExportPDF = () => {
> 577	    window.print();
> 578	  };
> 579	
> 580	  if (isLoading) {
> 581	    return (
> 582	      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-[calc(100vh-6rem)]">
> 583	        <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-primary" />
> 584	      </div>
> 585	    );
> 586	  }
> 587	
> 588	  if (!content) {
> 589	    return (
> 590	      <div className="p-6 text-center space-y-4">
> 591	        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Conteúdo não encontrado.</p>
> 592	        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/content')}>
> 593	          <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
> 594	          Voltar
> 595	        </Button>
> 596	      </div>
> 597	    );
> 598	  }
> 599	
> 600	  const totalDuration = scenes.reduce((acc, s) => {
> 601	    const match = s.duration.match(/(\d+)/);
> 602	    return acc + (match ? parseInt(match[1]) : 0);
> 603	  }, 0);
> 604	
> 605	  return (
> 606	    <>
> 607	      <style>{`
> 608	        @media print {
> 609	          body * { visibility: hidden; }
> 610	          .print-area, .print-area * { visibility: visible; }
> 611	          .print-area { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100%; }
> 612	          .no-print { display: none !important; }
> 613	        }
> 614	      `}</style>
> 615	
> 616	      <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-4">
> 617	        {/* Header */}
> 618	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2 no-print">
> 619	          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
> 620	            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/content')}>
> 621	              <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
> 622	            </Button>
> 623	            <div>
> 624	              <h1 className="text-xl font-heading font-bold text-foreground">{content.title || 'Editor de Roteiro'}</h1>
> 625	              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
> 626	                {scenes.length} cenas · ~{totalDuration}s total
> 627	              </p>
> 628	            </div>
> 629	          </div>
> 630	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
> 631	            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={handleExportPDF} className="gap-1">
> 632	              <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" />
> 633	              Exportar PDF
> 634	            </Button>
> 635	            <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} className="gap-1">
> 636	              {saving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 637	              Salvar
> 638	            </Button>
> 639	          </div>
> 640	        </div>
> 641	
> 642	        {/* ====== BLOCO CINEMA — Video Status / Player ====== */}
> 643	        <div className="rounded-lg border border-accent/30 bg-accent/5 p-4 space-y-3 no-print">
> 644	          <Label className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-accent flex items-center gap-1.5">
> 645	            <Film className="h-3 w-3" />
> 646	            Cinema — Status do Vídeo
> 647	          </Label>
> 648	
> 649	          {/* Ready — Video Player */}
> 650	          {videoUrl && (
> 651	            <div className="space-y-2">
> 652	              <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-hidden bg-black">
> 653	                <StorageVideo src={videoUrl} controls className="w-full max-h-[400px]" />
> 654	              </div>
> 655	              <p className="text-xs text-green-400 text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
> 656	                <Play className="h-3 w-3" /> Vídeo renderizado e disponível
> 657	              </p>
> 658	            </div>
> 659	          )}
> 660	
> 661	          {/* Asset history — todos os vídeos gerados para este conteúdo */}
> 662	          {assets.filter((a) => a.kind === 'video' && a.url).length > 0 && (
> 663	            <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-accent/20 pt-3">
> 664	              <Label className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-accent/80">
> 665	                Histórico de Assets ({assets.filter((a) => a.kind === 'video' && a.url).length})
> 666	              </Label>
> 667	              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
> 668	                {assets.filter((a) => a.kind === 'video' && a.url).map((a, i) => (
> 669	                  <button
> 670	                    key={`${a.url}-${i}`}
> 671	                    onClick={() => a.url && setVideoUrl(a.url)}
> 672	                    className="group relative rounded-md overflow-hidden border border-border hover:border-accent transition"
> 673	                    title={a.created_at ? new Date(a.created_at).toLocaleString('pt-BR') : ''}
> 674	                  >
> 675	                    <StorageVideo src={a.url} className="w-full h-20 object-cover" muted preload="metadata" />
> 676	                    <span className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 bg-black/60 text-[9px] text-white px-1 py-0.5 truncate">
> 677	                      {a.model || a.provider || 'vídeo'}
> 678	                    </span>
> 679	                  </button>
> 680	                ))}
> 681	              </div>
> 682	            </div>
> 683	          )}
> 684	
> 685	          {/* Processing — Progress Bar */}
> 686	          {videoRendering && (
> 687	            <div className="space-y-2">
> 688	              <div className="w-full bg-muted rounded-full h-2.5 overflow-hidden">
> 689	                <div
> 690	                  className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-primary to-accent rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 ease-out"
> 691	                  style={{ width: `${videoProgress}%` }}
> 692	                />
> 693	              </div>
> 694	              <p className="text-xs text-center text-muted-foreground flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
> 695	                <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" />
> 696	                {videoRenderStatus} ({videoProgress}%)
> 697	              </p>
> 698	              {operationId && (

> 701	                </p>
> 702	              )}
> 703	            </div>
> 704	          )}
> 705	
> 706	          {/* No video yet — offer render */}

> 718	              </Button>
> 719	              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
> 720	                O roteiro será enviado ao provedor de IA para gerar o vídeo
> 721	              </p>
> 722	            </div>
> 723	          )}
> 724	        </div>
> 725	
> 726	        {/* ====== BLOCO CÉREBRO — Scene Editor ====== */}
> 727	        <div className="rounded-lg border border-primary/30 bg-primary/5 p-4 space-y-3">
> 728	          <Label className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-4">
> 729	            <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3" />
> 730	            Cérebro — Gerador de Roteiro
> 731	          </Label>
> 732	
> 733	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
> 734	            <div>
> 735	              <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Provedor</Label>
> 736	              <Select value={videoProvider} onValueChange={(v) => { setVideoProvider(v); setVideoModel((TEXT_MODELS[v] || [])[0]?.value || ''); }}>
> 737	                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>

> 739	                  {VIDEO_PROVIDERS.map((p) => (

> 741	                  ))}

> 745	            <div>
> 746	              <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
> 747	                Modelo (texto)
> 748	              </Label>
> 749	              <Select value={videoModel} onValueChange={setVideoModel}>
> 750	                <SelectTrigger>
> 751	                  <SelectValue placeholder="Selecione um modelo de texto..." />
> 752	                </SelectTrigger>
> 753	                <SelectContent>
> 754	                  {(TEXT_MODELS[videoProvider] || []).map((m) => (

> 756	                  ))}
> 757	                </SelectContent>
> 758	              </Select>
> 759	            </div>
> 760	            
> 761	            <div>
> 762	              <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Formato</Label>
> 763	              <Select value={videoFormat} onValueChange={setVideoFormat}>
> 764	                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
> 765	                <SelectContent>
> 766	                  {VIDEO_FORMATS.map((f) => (
> 767	                    <SelectItem key={f.value} value={f.value}>{f.label}</SelectItem>
> 768	                  ))}
> 769	                </SelectContent>
> 770	              </Select>
> 771	            </div>
> 772	            <div>
> 773	              <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Duração</Label>
> 774	              <Select value={videoDuration} onValueChange={setVideoDuration}>
> 775	                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
> 776	                <SelectContent>
> 777	                  {VIDEO_DURATIONS.map((d) => (

> 779	                  ))}
> 780	                </SelectContent>
> 781	              </Select>
> 782	            </div>
> 783	          </div>
> 784	
> 785	          <div className="mb-4">
> 786	            <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Prompt do Roteiro</Label>
> 787	            <Textarea
> 788	              value={videoPrompt}

> 791	              rows={3}
> 792	              className="text-sm"
> 793	            />
> 794	          </div>
> 795	          <Button
> 796	            onClick={handleGenerateScript}
> 797	            disabled={scriptGenerating || !videoPrompt.trim()}
> 798	            className="w-full gap-2 mb-6"
> 799	          >
> 800	            {scriptGenerating ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 801	            {scriptGenerating ? "Gerando Cenas..." : "Gerar Roteiro (LLM)"}
> 802	          </Button>
> 803	
> 804	          <Label className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-6 mb-2 pt-4 border-t border-primary/30">
> 805	            <Type className="h-3 w-3" />
> 806	            Cenas ({scenes.length})

> 820	                  {i < scenes.length - 1 && <div className="w-4 h-px bg-border" />}

> 824	          </div>
> 825

> 829	              <SortableContext items={scenes.map((s) => s.id)} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>

> 832	                ))}

> 834	            </DndContext>
> 835	
> 836	            {scenes.length === 0 && (

> 838	                <CardContent className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-center">

> 840	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">

> 843	                </CardContent>
> 844	              </Card>
> 845	            )}
> 846	          </div>
> 847	        </div>
> 848	      </div>

> **Tool Result (Read)** - ✅ Success
>
> ```
> 1	import {
> 2	  Rocket, PenLine, Image, Megaphone, ShieldCheck, Globe, Briefcase, Send,
> 3	  Bot, Clapperboard, Radio, Zap, Target, type LucideIcon,
> 4	} from 'lucide-react';
> 5	
> 6	// MIV: node identity via line-icon (not emoji). `icon` is a serializable string name persisted
> 7	// in the graph JSON; this maps it to a lucide component. Unknown names fall back to raw text so
> 8	// legacy graphs saved with emoji still render.
> 9	const MAP: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
> 10	  rocket: Rocket,
> 11	  'pen-line': PenLine,
> 12	  image: Image,
> 13	  megaphone: Megaphone,
> 14	  'shield-check': ShieldCheck,
> 15	  globe: Globe,
> 16	  briefcase: Briefcase,
> 17	  twitter: Send,
> 18	  bot: Bot,
> 19	  clapperboard: Clapperboard,
> 20	  radio: Radio,
> 21	  zap: Zap,
> 22	  target: Target,
> 23	};
> 24	
> 25	export function NodeIcon({ name, className }: { name?: string; className?: string }) {
> 26	  const Icon = name ? MAP[name] : undefined;
> 27	  if (Icon) return <Icon className={className} />;
> 28	  // Legacy fallback: render the raw string (e.g. an emoji from a previously-saved graph).
> 29	  return <span className={className}>{name}</span>;
> 30	}
> 31	
> ```

> 141	  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);

> 440	            </div>
> 441	          </CardContent>
> 442	        </Card>
> 443	      </motion.div>
> 444	
> 445	      {/* Self-Optimization + Self-Healing row */}
> 446	      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
> 447	        {/* Self-Optimization Widget */}
> 448	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.28 }}>
> 449	          <Card className="glass-panel border-primary/20 h-full">
> 450	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
> 451	              <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 452	                <Brain className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
> 453	                Auto-otimização IA

> 455	              </CardTitle>
> 456	            </CardHeader>
> 457	            <CardContent>
> 458	              {!optResult ? (
> 459	                <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-6 gap-4">
> 460	                  <Sparkles className="h-10 w-10 text-primary/60" />
> 461	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center max-w-md">
> 462	                    Analise suas métricas com IA para recomendações personalizadas.
> 463	                  </p>
> 464	                  <Button onClick={runOptimization} disabled={optimizing} className="gap-2">
> 465	                    {optimizing ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Brain className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 466	                    {optimizing ? 'Analisando...' : 'Analisar e Otimizar'}
> 467	                  </Button>
> 468	                </div>
> 469	              ) : (
> 470	                <div className="space-y-4">
> 471	                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
> 472	                    <div className="text-center">
> 473	                      <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-primary">{optResult.score}</p>
> 474	                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Score</p>
> 475	                    </div>
> 476	                    <div className="flex-1">
> 477	                      <Progress value={optResult.score} className="h-2" />
> 478	                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{optResult.summary}</p>
> 479	                    </div>
> 480	                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={runOptimization} disabled={optimizing} className="gap-1">
> 481	                      {optimizing ? <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" /> : <ArrowUpRight className="h-3 w-3" />}
> 482	                      Reanalisar
> 483	                    </Button>
> 484	                  </div>
> 485	                  <div className="space-y-2">
> 486	                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Recomendações</p>
> 487	                    {optResult.recommendations.slice(0, 4).map((rec, i) => (
> 488	                      <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 text-sm">
> 489	                        <span className="text-xs mt-0.5">{CATEGORY_LABELS[rec.category] || rec.category}</span>
> 490	                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 491	                          <p className="font-medium text-foreground text-xs">{rec.title}</p>
> 492	                          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{rec.description}</p>
> 493	                        </div>
> 494	                        <Badge variant="outline" className={`text-[10px] ${IMPACT_COLORS[rec.impact] || ''}`}>
> 495	                          {rec.impact}
> 496	                        </Badge>
> 497	                      </div>
> 498	                    ))}
> 499	                  </div>
> 500	                </div>
> 501	              )}
> 502	            </CardContent>
> 503	          </Card>
> 504	        </motion.div>
> 505	
> 506	        {/* Self-Healing Widget */}
> 507	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.32 }}>
> 508	          <Card className="glass-panel border-accent/20 h-full">
> 509	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
> 510	              <CardTitle className="font-heading text-base flex items-center gap-2">
> 511	                <HeartPulse className="h-4 w-4 text-accent" />
> 512	                Self-Healing
> 513	                <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-[10px]">Fase 17</Badge>
> 514	              </CardTitle>
> 515	            </CardHeader>
> 516	            <CardContent>
> 517	              {!healResult ? (
> 518	                <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-6 gap-4">
> 519	                  <Shield className="h-10 w-10 text-accent/60" />
> 520	                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center max-w-md">
> 521	                    Detecta posts falhos, tokens expirados e publicações travadas — corrige automaticamente.
> 522	                  </p>

> 524	                    {healing ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <HeartPulse className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 525	                    {healing ? 'Diagnosticando...' : 'Executar Diagnóstico'}
> 526	                  </Button>
> 527	                </div>

> 529	                <div className="space-y-4">

> 539	                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Resolvidos</p>
> 540	                      </div>
> 541	                      <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10">
> 542	                        <p className="text-xl font-bold text-destructive">{healResult.needs_attention}</p>
> 543	                        <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">Pendentes</p>
> 544	                      </div>
> 545	                    </div>
> 546	                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={runHealing} disabled={healing} className="gap-1">
> 547	                      {healing ? <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" /> : <RefreshCw className="h-3 w-3" />}
> 548	                      Rediagnosticar
> 549	                    </Button>
> 550	                  </div>
> 551	
> 552	                  {/* Actions */}
> 553	                  {healResult.total_issues === 0 ? (
> 554	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-green-500/10 text-sm">
> 555	                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-green-400" />
> 556	                      <span className="text-green-400 font-medium">Tudo funcionando corretamente!</span>
> 557	                    </div>
> 558	                  ) : (
> 559	                    <div className="space-y-2 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
> 560	                      {healResult.actions.map((action, i) => (
> 561	                        <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 text-xs">
> 562	                          {action.success ? (
> 563	                            <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-green-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
> 564	                          ) : (
> 565	                            <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-destructive mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
> 566	                          )}
> 567	                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 568	                            <p className="font-medium text-foreground">{action.issue_details}</p>
> 569	                            <p className="text-muted-foreground">{action.action_taken}</p>
> 570	                          </div>
> 571	                        </div>
> 572	                      ))}
> 573	                    </div>
> 574	                  )}
> 575	                </div>
> 576	              )}
> 577	            </CardContent>
> 578	          </Card>
> 579	        </motion.div>
> 580	      </div>
> 581	
> 582	      {/* Optimization History */}
> 583	      {optHistory.length > 0 && (
> 584	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.36 }}>

> 595	                {optHistory.map((item) => (

> 598	                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">

> 603	                      <Badge

> 605	                        className={`text-[10px] ${item.applied ? 'text-green-400 bg-green-400/10' : 'text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50'}`}

> 607	                        {item.applied ? 'Aplicado' : 'Pendente'}
> 608	                      </Badge>

> 621	      {/* Realtime Content Library Section */}
> 622	      <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.38 }}>
> 623	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">

> 625	            <Library className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />

> 636	        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
> 637	          {loadingContent ? (

> 640	            ))

> 647	            contentItems.map((item, index) => (

> 662	                        {item.category}
> 663	                      </Badge>

> 669	                        {item.category}
> 670	                      </Badge>

> 672	                  )}
> 673	                  
> 674	                  <CardContent className="p-4 flex-1 flex flex-col">
> 675	                    <h4 className="font-heading font-bold text-sm text-foreground line-clamp-1 mb-2">
> 676	                      {item.title}
> 677	                    </h4>
> 678	                    {/* content_html = content_library.body (raw AI-generated HTML); DOMPurify sanitizes to close the stored-XSS sink while preserving standard formatting + affiliate <a href> links */}
> 679	                    <div 
> 680	                      className="text-xs text-muted-foreground line-clamp-2 mb-4 flex-1"
> 681	                      // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- HTML sanitized via DOMPurify (content_library.body stored-XSS sink); see src/test/dashboard-html-sanitize.test.ts
> 682	                      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: DOMPurify.sanitize(item.content_html) }}
> 683	                    />
> 684	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-auto pt-3 border-t border-primary/10">
> 685	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1">

> 687	                        {format(parseISO(item.created_at), "dd MMM", { locale: ptBR })}
> 688	                      </span>

> 690	                        <ArrowUpRight className="h-3 w-3" />
> 691	                      </Button>
> 692	                    </div>
> 693	                  </CardContent>
> 694	                </motion.div>
> 695	                {(index + 1) % 2 === 0 && <AdPlaceholder />}
> 696	              </div>
> 697	            ))
> 698	          )}
> 699	        </div>

> 701	
> 702	      {/* Charts row */}
> 703	
> 704	      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
> 705	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.3 }} className="lg:col-span-2">
> 706	          <Card className="glass-panel">

> 711	              </CardTitle>
> 712	            </CardHeader>
> 713	            <CardContent>
> 714	              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
> 715	                <AreaChart data={engagementData}>
> 716	                  <defs>
> 717	                    <linearGradient id="fillEngagement" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
> 718	                      <stop offset="0%" stopColor="hsl(185, 80%, 55%)" stopOpacity={0.3} />
> 719	                      <stop offset="100%" stopColor="hsl(185, 80%, 55%)" stopOpacity={0} />
> 720	                    </linearGradient>

> 724	                  <YAxis stroke="hsl(215, 20%, 55%)" fontSize={12} />
> 725	                  <Tooltip contentStyle={{ backgroundColor: 'hsl(222, 40%, 10%)', border: '1px solid hsl(222, 30%, 25%)', borderRadius: '8px', color: 'hsl(210, 40%, 92%)', fontSize: '12px' }} />
> 726	                  <Area type="monotone" dataKey="engajamento" stroke="hsl(185, 80%, 55%)" fill="url(#fillEngagement)" strokeWidth={2} />
> 727	                </AreaChart>
> 728	              </ResponsiveContainer>
> 729	            </CardContent>
> 730	          </Card>

> 732	
> 733	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.4 }}>
> 734	          <Card className="glass-panel h-full">
> 735	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">

> 738	                Por Plataforma

> 740	            </CardHeader>

> 742	              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={170}>

> 744	                  <Pie data={platformData} cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius={45} outerRadius={70} paddingAngle={3} dataKey="value">
> 745	                    {platformData.map((_, i) => (
> 746	                      <Cell key={i} fill={PIE_COLORS[i]} />
> 747	                    ))}
> 748	                  </Pie>
> 749	                  <Tooltip contentStyle={{ backgroundColor: 'hsl(222, 40%, 10%)', border: '1px solid hsl(222, 30%, 25%)', borderRadius: '8px', color: 'hsl(210, 40%, 92%)', fontSize: '12px' }} formatter={(value: number) => [`${value}%`, 'Share']} />
> 750	                </PieChart>
> 751	              </ResponsiveContainer>
> 752	              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2 justify-center">
> 753	                {platformData.map((p, i) => (
> 754	                  <div key={p.name} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
> 755	                    <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: PIE_COLORS[i] }} />
> 756	                    {p.name}
> 757	                  </div>
> 758	                ))}
> 759	              </div>
> 760	            </CardContent>
> 761	          </Card>
> 762	        </motion.div>
> 763	      </div>
> 764	
> 765	      <ROIWidget />
> 766	
> 767	      <PantheonTelemetryWidget />
> 768	
> 769	      <ConstellationWidget />
> 770	
> 771	      {upcomingPosts && upcomingPosts.length > 0 && (

> 773	          <Card className="glass-panel">

> 779	                  {upcomingPosts.filter(p => p.status === 'queued').length} na fila
> 780	                </Badge>
> 781	              </CardTitle>
> 782	            </CardHeader>
> 783	            <CardContent>
> 784	              <div className="space-y-2">
> 785	                {upcomingPosts.slice(0, 5).map((post) => (
> 786	                  <div key={post.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm p-2 rounded-md bg-secondary/30">
> 787	                    <div className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${
> 788	                      post.status === 'published' ? 'bg-green-500' :
> 789	                      post.status === 'failed' ? 'bg-destructive' :
> 790	                      post.status === 'publishing' ? 'bg-amber-500 animate-pulse' :
> 791	                      'bg-primary'
> 792	                    }`} />
> 793	                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
> 794	                      <span className="text-foreground font-medium capitalize text-xs">{post.platform}</span>
> 795	                      <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
> 796	                        {format(parseISO(post.scheduled_at), "dd/MM 'às' HH:mm", { locale: ptBR })}
> 797	                      </p>
> 798	                    </div>
> 799	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
> 800	                      {post.status === 'published' && <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3 text-green-500" />}
> 801	                      {post.status === 'failed' && <AlertTriangle className="h-3 w-3 text-destructive" />}
> 802	                      <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px]">{post.status}</Badge>
> 803	                    </div>
> 804	                  </div>
> 805	                ))}

> 807	            </CardContent>
> 808	          </Card>

> 813	      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
> 814	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.5 }}>
> 815	          <Card className="glass-panel">

> 845	        <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.55 }}>
> 846	          <Card className="glass-panel">
> 847	            <CardHeader className="pb-2">

> 265	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border/30 pt-3 mt-1">

> 267	            size="sm"
> 268	            variant="ghost"

> 277	                <ToggleRight className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />

> 282	                <ToggleLeft className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
> 283	                <span>Ativar</span>
> 284	              </>
> 285	            )}
> 286	          </Button>
> 287	
> 288	          <Button
> 289	            size="sm"
> 290	            variant="ghost"
> 291	            onClick={() => onDelete(product.id)}
> 292	            disabled={isDeleting}
> 293	            className="text-xs text-destructive hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive flex items-center gap-1"
> 294	          >
> 295	            {isDeleting ? (
> 296	              <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
> 297	            ) : (
> 298	              <>
> 299	                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
> 300	                <span>Excluir</span>
> 301	              </>
> 302	            )}
> 303	          </Button>
> 304	        </div>
> 305	      </CardContent>
> 306	
> 307	      <Dialog open={dialogOpen} onOpenChange={setDialogOpen}>
> 308	        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
> 309	          <DialogHeader>
> 310	            <DialogTitle>Link cravado ({product.network === 'mercadolivre' ? 'meli.la' : 'mpago.li'})</DialogTitle>
> 311	            <DialogDescription>
> 312	              Cole o short link gerado no painel do {product.network === 'mercadolivre' ? 'Mercado Livre' : 'Mercado Pago'} para <strong>{product.name}</strong>. Ele garante
> 313	              atribuição cravada (conta + canal). {product.network === 'mercadolivre' ? 'Sem ele, o sistema usa o link automático com sua etiqueta matt_word.' : ''}
> 314	            </DialogDescription>
> 315	          </DialogHeader>
> 316	          <Input
> 317	            value={draft}
> 318	            onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
> 319	            placeholder={product.network === 'mercadolivre' ? 'https://meli.la/2gh13Sw' : 'https://mpago.li/2GZ78H'}
> 320	            autoFocus
> 321	          />
> 322	          {!configReady && product.network === 'mercadolivre' && (
> 323	            <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-500">
> 324	              Configure suas credenciais ML em /dashboard/affiliates antes de salvar.
> 325	            </p>
> 326	          )}
> 327	          <DialogFooter className="gap-2 sm:gap-2">
> 328	            {existingShortlink && (
> 329	              <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => saveShortlink(null)} disabled={isSaving}>
> 330	                Remover
> 331	              </Button>
> 332	            )}
> 333	            <Button onClick={() => saveShortlink(draft)} disabled={isSaving || (product.network === 'mercadolivre' && !configReady) || !draft.trim()}>
> 334	              {isSaving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : 'Salvar'}
> 335	            </Button>
> 336	          </DialogFooter>
> 337	        </DialogContent>
> 338	      </Dialog>
> 339	    </Card>
> 340	  );
> 341	}

> 471	  const filteredHistory = useMemo(() => {
> 472	    let result = [...minerHistory];

> 474	    // Search filter

> 476	    if (searchVal) {
> 477	      result = result.filter(

> 489	    }
> 490	
> 491	    // Sorting
> 492	    result.sort((a, b) => {
> 493	      let aVal: any = a[historySortField];
> 494	      let bVal: any = b[historySortField];
> 495	
> 496	      if (historySortField === 'created_at') {

> 500	
> 501	      if (aVal < bVal) return historySortOrder === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
> 502	      if (aVal > bVal) return historySortOrder === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
> 503	      return 0;
> 504	    });
> 505	
> 506	    return result;
> 507	  }, [minerHistory, historySearch, historyModelFilter, historySortField, historySortOrder]);

> 517	  // Actions implementations
> 518	  const handleDeleteProduct = async (id: string) => {

> 536	  const handleToggleProductActive = async (id: string, currentStatus: boolean) => {
> 537	    setTogglingId(id);
> 538	    try {

> 541	      });
> 542	      if (error || data?.error) throw new Error(data?.error || "Falha ao alterar status");
> 543	      toast.success(`Produto ${!currentStatus ? 'ativado' : 'desativado'} com sucesso!`);
> 544	      refetchCatalog();
> 545	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['affiliate_products'] });
> 546	    } catch (err: any) {

> 548	    } finally {
> 549	      setTogglingId(null);
> 550	    }
> 551	  };
> 552	
> 553	  const cleanAndParseJson = (text: string) => {
> 554	    let clean = (text || "").trim();
> 555	    if (clean.startsWith("```")) {
> 556	      clean = clean.replace(/^```(json)?\s*/i, "").replace(/```\s*$/i, "").trim();
> 557	    }
> 558	    // Isolate the JSON object — free models often wrap it in prose.
> 559	    const first = clean.indexOf("{");
> 560	    const last = clean.lastIndexOf("}");
> 561	    const candidate = first !== -1 && last > first ? clean.slice(first, last + 1) : clean;
> 562	
> 563	    // 1) Strict parse (the happy path).
> 564	    try {
> 565	      return JSON.parse(candidate);
> 566	    } catch {
> 567	      // 2) Tolerant field extraction — survives unescaped quotes / truncation in the long product_reasoning
> 568	      // value. search_keyword + recommended_niche come BEFORE it, so they're recoverable even when the
> 569	      // overall JSON is malformed. This keeps "Analisar Contexto" from failing on imperfect model output.
> 570	      const KEYS = 'search_keyword|recommended_niche|confidence|product_reasoning';
> 571	      const grab = (key: string) => {
> 572	        // Standard JSON: "key": "value..."
> 573	        const q = candidate.match(new RegExp(`"${key}"\\s*:\\s*"((?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*)"`));
> 574	        if (q) return q[1].replace(/\\"/g, '"').trim();
> 575	        // Loose: free models sometimes drop the quotes entirely (key: value, key: value). Capture the value
> 576	        // up to the next KNOWN key or the closing brace (so a reasoning with commas still parses).
> 577	        const u = candidate.match(new RegExp(`"?${key}"?\\s*:\\s*"?(.*?)"?\\s*(?:,\\s*"?(?:${KEYS})"?\\s*:|}\\s*$)`, 'is'));
> 578	        return u ? u[1].replace(/^["']+|["']+$/g, '').trim() : '';
> 579	      };
> 580	      const search_keyword = grab("search_keyword");
> 581	      if (!search_keyword) {
> 582	        console.error("Failed to parse synthesis JSON:", text);
> 583	        throw new Error("Resposta da IA em formato inválido.");
> 584	      }
> 585	      return {
> 586	        search_keyword,
> 587	        recommended_niche: grab("recommended_niche"),
> 588	        confidence: grab("confidence"),
> 589	        product_reasoning: grab("product_reasoning") || clean.slice(0, 600),
> 590	      };
> 591	    }
> 592	  };
> 593	
> 594	  // Async miner job: kick the TIMEOUT-PROOF background pipeline (Apify extract + Vision MCP) and watch it
> 595	  // via Realtime. Variable video size/length is absorbed by the background task — the click returns instantly,
> 596	  // a progress bar tracks the stages, and a notification fires when it finishes (no gateway timeout possible).
> 597	  const extractKickAtRef = useRef(0);
> 598	  const { job: minerJob, isMining, startMining } = useMinerJob({
> 599	    onDone: (row) => {
> 600	      setCustomInput(row.context_text || '');
> 601	      if (row.vision_analysis) {
> 602	        setMcpVisualDescription(row.vision_analysis);
> 603	        setMcpCostCoins(2.0);
> 604	        setMcpRealCostUsd(0.0054);
> 605	      }
> 606	      setMcpExtractLatency(extractKickAtRef.current ? Math.round(performance.now() - extractKickAtRef.current) : 0);
> 607	      toast.success(`Análise concluída (${row.vision_source === 'cover' ? 'imagem de capa' : 'vídeo'})!`, { id: 'extract-loader' });
> 608	    },
> 609	    onError: (row) => {
> 610	      toast.error(`Falha na extração: ${row.error || 'erro'}`, { id: 'extract-loader' });
> 611	      if (selectedTrendId) {
> 612	        const trend = trends.find((t: any) => t.id === selectedTrendId);
> 613	        if (trend) {
> 614	          setCustomInput(`Título: ${trend.title}\nDescrição: ${trend.description || ''}\n(Extração ao vivo falhou — ${row.error || ''})`);
> 615	          toast.info('Usando dados do cache local como alternativa.');
> 616	        }
> 617	      }
> 618	    },
> 619	  });
> 620	
> 621	  const handleExtractMetadata = async () => {
> 622	    let urlToScrape = customLink.trim();
> 623	    let niche = 'geral';

> 626	    if (selectedTrendId) {

> 640	      return;
> 641	    }

> 643	    setCustomInput('');

> 656	    }
> 657	  };
> 658	
> 659	  const handleAnalyzeTrend = async () => {

> 662	
> 663	    if (selectedTrendId && !textToAnalyze) {

> 670	
> 671	    if (!textToAnalyze) {

> 673	      return;
> 674	    }
> 675	
> 676	    if (swarmModels.length === 0) {
> 677	      toast.error("Por favor, ative ao menos um modelo no Swarm Selector antes de analisar.");
> 678	      return;
> 679	    }
> 680	
> 681	    setIsAnalyzing(true);
> 682	    setSearchKeyword('');
